%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02377500
%%                                                                 %%             02377510
%%               R E S P A L D O         F I L E S                 %%             02377520
%%                     C O R P O R A T I V O                       %%             02377530
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02377540
                                                                                  02377550
SUBROUTINE RESPFILES;                                                             02377600
BEGIN                                                                             02377700
  TASK COPIA;                                                                     02377800
                                                                                  02377900
ON RESTART,                                                                       02378000
   BEGIN                                                                          02378100
   MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "I>020102<" & "CAIDA DEL EQUIPO, MONTAR CINTAS"            02378200
             &" PARA REPETIR RESP FILES S151";                                    02378300
   WAIT (MENSAJE,OK);                                                             02378400
   GO RESPFILESS;                                                                 02378500
   END;                                                                           02378600
ON TASKFAULT,                                                                     02378700
   BEGIN                                                                          02378800
   OPCION := "";                                                                  02378900
   WHILE  OPCION NEQ "SI"                                                         02379000
          AND OPCION NEQ "NO" AND OPCION NEQ "RD" DO                              02379100
      BEGIN                                                                       02379200
      MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "O>030403<" & "FALLA RESPALDANDO FILES S151"            02379300
               & ", 'SI' = REINTENTO, 'NO' = CONTINUAR WFL"                       02379400
               & ", 'RD' = RESPALDO A DISCO";                                     02379500
      OPCION  := ACCEPT (MENSAJE);                                                02379600
      OPCION  := HEAD(TAIL(OPCION," "),NOT " ");                                  02379700
      END;                                                                        02379800
   IF OPCION = "RD" THEN                                                          02379900
    BEGIN                                                                         02380000
      RESPDISK:= "SI";                                                            02380100
      FAMRESP:=ACCEPT("DAME EL NOMBRE DE LA FAMILIA PARA RESPALDAR");             02380200
      GO RESPFILESS                                                               02380300
    END;                                                                          02380400
   IF OPCION = "SI" THEN                                                          02380500
      GO RESPFILESS                                                               02380600
   ELSE                                                                           02380700
      GO TERMINARESPFILES;                                                        02380800
   END;                                                                           02380900
RESPFILESS:                                                                       02381000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020101<"                                                      02381100
       &  "INICIA RESP FILES S151";                                               02381200
BEGIN                                                                             02381300
 IF (COPIA ISNT COMPLETEDOK OR COPIA(VALUE) = 1) AND                              02381400
      COPIA ISNT ACTIVE THEN                                                      02381500
  IF RESPDISK = "SI" THEN                                                         02381600
  PROCESS COPY S084/FILE/E01/=                                                    02381700
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02381800
              &"/S084/FILE/E01/=")  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                          02381900
              ,S087/FILE/E01/=                                                    02382000
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02382100
              &"/S087/FILE/E01/=")  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                          02382200
              ,S408/FILE/E01/=                                                    02382300
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02382400
              &"/S408/FILE/E01/=")  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                          02382500
              ,S500/FILE/E01/=                                                    02382600
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02382700
              &"/S500/FILE/E01/=")  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                          02382800
              ,S701/FILE/E01/=                                                    02382900
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02383000
              &"/S701/FILE/E01/=")  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                          02383100
              ,S151/FILE/E01/=                                                    02383200
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02383300
              &"/S151/FILE/E01/=")  FROM #PACK1(PACK)                             02383400
              ,S151/FILE/E01/=                                                    02383500
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02383600
              &"/S151/FILE/E01/=")  FROM #PACK2(PACK)                             02383700
              ,S084/LIST/P138/=                                                   02383800
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02383900
              &"/S084/LIST/P138/=")  FROM PACK(PACK)                              02384000
              ,S087/LIST/P138/=                                                   02384100
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02384200
              &"/S087/LIST/P138/=")  FROM PACK(PACK)                              02384300
              ,S408/LIST/P138/=                                                   02384400
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02384500
              &"/S408/LIST/P138/=")  FROM PACK(PACK)                              02384600
              ,S500/LIST/P138/=                                                   02384700
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02384800
              &"/S500/LIST/P138/=")  FROM PACK(PACK)                              02384900
              ,S701/LIST/P138/=                                                   02385000
              AS #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP                                        02385100
              &"/S701/LIST/P138/=")  FROM PACK(PACK)                              02385200
      TO #FAMRESP (PACK)[COPIA];                                                  02385300
  ELSE                                                                            02385400
  PROCESS COPY S084/FILE/E01/=  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                              02385500
              ,S087/FILE/E01/=  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                              02385600
              ,S408/FILE/E01/=  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                              02385700
              ,S500/FILE/E01/=  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                              02385800
              ,S701/FILE/E01/=  FROM CAPTACION(PACK)                              02385900
              ,S151/FILE/E01/=  FROM #PACK1(PACK)                                 02386000
              ,S151/FILE/E01/=  FROM #PACK2(PACK)                                 02386100
              ,S084/LIST/P138/= FROM PACK(PACK)                                   02386200
              ,S087/LIST/P138/= FROM PACK(PACK)                                   02386300
              ,S408/LIST/P138/= FROM PACK(PACK)                                   02386400
              ,S500/LIST/P138/= FROM PACK(PACK)                                   02386500
              ,S701/LIST/P138/= FROM PACK(PACK)                                   02386600
      TO #("S151FIOBDIA" & FECCORP)[COPIA];                                       02386700
 END;                                                                             02386800
FINRESPFILES: WAIT(COPIA);                                                        02386900
TERMINARESPFILES:                                                                 02387000
 DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020201<" & "TERMINA RESP FILES S151";                        02387100
END;                                                                              02387200
                                                                                  02464000

%%                    B A J A      F I L E S                       %% V           02512000
%%                                                                 %% V           02513000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02514000
                                                                                  02515000
SUBROUTINE BAJAFILESSUB;                                                          02516000
BEGIN                                                                             02517000
ON RESTART, GO BAJAA;                                                             02518000
ON TASKFAULT,                                                                     02519000
   BEGIN                                                                          02520000
   OPCION := "";                                                                  02521000
   WHILE  OPCION NEQ "SI"                                                         02522000
          AND OPCION NEQ "NO" DO                                                  02523000
      BEGIN                                                                       02524000
      MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "O>030403<" & "FALLA AL BAJAR FILES S151"               02525000
               & ", 'SI' = REINTENTO, 'NO' = TERMINAR WFL";                       02526000
      OPCION  := ACCEPT (MENSAJE);                                                02527000
      OPCION  := HEAD(TAIL(OPCION," "),NOT " ");                                  02528000
      END;                                                                        02529000
   IF OPCION = "SI" THEN                                                          02530000
      GO BAJAA;                                                                   02531000
   ELSE                                                                           02532000
      GO  FINBAJAA;                                                               02533000
   END;                                                                           02534000
BAJAA:                                                                            02535000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020101<"                                                      02536000
       & "INICIA BAJA FILES DE S151";                                             02537000
JOBATRIBUTOS;                                                                     02538000
COPY S151/FILE/= FROM S151FIANTERIOR TO #PACK1(PACK);                             02539000
  FINBAJAA:                                                                       02540000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020201<"                                                      02541000
      & "TERMINA BAJA FILES DE S151";                                             02542000
END;                                                                              02543000

SUBROUTINE SUBEPROGS;                                                             02766000
BEGIN                                                                             02767000
ON RESTART,                                                                       02768000
   BEGIN                                                                          02769000
   MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "I>020501" & "CAIDA DEL EQUIPO, MONTAR CINTAS"             02770000
             &" PARA REPETIR SUBE PROGRAMAS S151";                                02771000
   WAIT(MENSAJE,OK);                                                              02772000
   GO SUBEPROGSS;                                                                 02773000
   END;                                                                           02774000
ON TASKFAULT,                                                                     02775000
   BEGIN                                                                          02776000
   MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "I>020501<";                                               02777000
   MENSAJE := MENSAJE & "FALLA AL SUBIR PROGRAMAS S151, MONTAR CINTAS"            02778000
             &" PARA REINTENTAR";                                                 02779000
   WAIT(MENSAJE,OK);                                                              02780000
   GO SUBEPROGSS;                                                                 02781000
   END;                                                                           02782000
SUBEPROGSS:                                                                       02783000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020101<"                                                      02784000
       & "INICIA SUBE PROGRAMAS S151";                                            02785000
JOBATRIBUTOS;                                                                     02786000
COPY S151/WFL/=, S151/OBJECT/=                                                    02787000
    ,DESCRIPTION/=, DMSUPPORT/=,S151BD02ADSALDO/CONTROL                           02788000
    ,RECONSTRUCT/=  FROM #PACK1(PACK)                                             02789000
    TO #("S151PROLEVE" & STRING(FECHAY,6));                                       02790000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020201<"                                                      02791000
       &  "TERMINA SUBE PROGRAMAS S151";                                          02792000
END;                                                                              02793000

%%                     D B A N A L A Y Z E R                       %%             02797000
%%                                                                 %%             02798000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02799000
                                                                                  02800000
SUBROUTINE DBANALYZER;                                                            02801000
BEGIN                                                                             02802000
ON RESTART, GO DBANALYZE;                                                         02803000
ON TASKFAULT,                                                                     02804000
   BEGIN                                                                          02805000
   MENSAJE:= MSGHDR & "O>030406<" & "FALLA DBANALYZER, INDICA OPCION: "           02806000
            &"R=REINTENTO, C=CONTINUA WFL, T=TERMINA WFL";                        02807000
   DO BEGIN                                                                       02808000
      OPCION:= ACCEPT(MENSAJE);                                                   02809000
      OPCION:= HEAD(TAIL(OPCION," "),NOT " ")                                     02810000
      END UNTIL OPCION = "R" OR OPCION = "C"                                      02811000
                OR OPCION = "T";                                                  02812000
   IF OPCION = "R" THEN                                                           02813000
      GO DBANALYZE;                                                               02814000
   ELSE IF OPCION = "C" THEN                                                      02815000
      GO BACKFORZADBANALYZE;                                                      02816000
   ELSE IF OPCION = "T" THEN                                                      02817000
      GO FIN;                                                                     02818000
   END;                                                                           02819000
DBANALYZE:                                                                        02820000
JOBATRIBUTOS;                                                                     02821000
DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020101<"                                                      02842000
       & "INICIA DBANALYZER S151BD02ADSALDO";                                     02843000
RUN #("SYSTEM/DBANALYZER" ON DMSIIPACK);                                          02844000
BDNAME = S151/LIST/DBANALYZER/S151BD02ADSALDO;                                    02845000
FILE DASDL = DESCRIPTION/S151BD02ADSALDO;                                         02846000
DATA CARD                                                                         02847000
   ANALYZE ALL (OPTIONS = ALL);                                                   02848000
?                                                                                 02849000
 DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020201<"                                                     02850000
       & "TERMINA DBANALYZER S151BD02ADSALDO" ;                                   02851000
BACKFORZADBANALYZE:                                                               02852000
RUN SYSTEM/FORZA("S151/LIST/DBANALYZER");                                         02853000
END;                                                                              02854000
                                                                                  02855000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02978000

%%         R E B U I L D    S 1 5 1 B D 0 2 A D S A L D O          %%             02980000
%%                                                                 %%             02981000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%             02982000
                                                                                  02983000
SUBROUTINE REBUILDADS;                                                            02984000
BEGIN                                                                             02985000
ON RESTART, GO REBUIADS;                                                          02986000
ON TASKFAULT,                                                                     02987000
   BEGIN                                                                          02988000
   MENSAJE:= MSGHDR & "O>030406<" & "FALLA REBUILD, INDICA OPCION: "              02989000
            &"R=REINTENTO, C=CONTINUA WFL, T=TERMINA WFL";                        02990000
   DO BEGIN                                                                       02991000
      OPCION := ACCEPT(MENSAJE);                                                  02992000
      OPCION := HEAD(TAIL(OPCION," "),NOT " ")                                    02993000
      END UNTIL OPCION = "R" OR OPCION = "C"                                      02994000
          OR OPCION = "T";                                                        02995000
   IF OPCION = "R" THEN                                                           02996000
       BEGIN                                                                      02997000
       SEPARAPARAM;                                                               02998000
       GO REBUIADS;                                                               02999000
       END                                                                        03000000
   ELSE IF OPCION = "C" THEN                                                      03001000
       GO BACKFORZAREBADS                                                         03002000
   ELSE IF OPCION = "T" THEN                                                      03003000
       GO FIN;                                                                    03004000
   END;                                                                           03005000
REBUIADS:                                                                         03006000
 DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020101<"                                                     03007000
       & "INICIA REBUILD DE S151BD02ADSALDO";                                     03008000
 RUN #("SYSTEM/DMUTILITY" ON DMSIIPACK)                                           03009000
     ("DB = S151BD02ADSALDO  RECOVER (REBUILD THRU AUDIT "                        03010000
     & AUX1 & ") = FROM ADSALDOAUDIT ");                                          03011000
 BDNAME = S151/LIST/REBUILD/S151BD02ADSALDO ;                                     03012000
 DISPLAY MSGHDR & "I>020201<"                                                     03013000
       & "TERMINA REBUILD DE S151BD02ADSALDO";                                    03014000
BACKFORZAREBADS:                                                                  03015000
RUN SYSTEM/FORZA("S151/LIST/REBUILD/S151BD02ADSALDO");                            03016000
END;                                                                              03017000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                   03018000
%%                       PASO  P710                          %%                   03019000
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                   03020000
                                                                                  03021000
SUBROUTINE SUB710;                                                                03022000
 BEGIN                                                                            03023000
 ON RESTART, GO PASO710;                                                          03024000
 ON TASKFAULT,                                                                    03025000
  BEGIN                                                                           03026000
   MENSAJE := MSGHDR & "O>030406< FALLA DE PASO 710/E " & AUX1/AUX2               03027000
       & " , INDICA OPCION: R=REINTENTO, C=CONTINUA WFL, T=FIN WFL";              03028000
   DO BEGIN                                                                       03029000
     OPCION := ACCEPT(MENSAJE); OPCION:=HEAD(TAIL(OPCION," "),NOT " ");           03030000
   END UNTIL OPCION = "R" OR OPCION = "C" OR OPCION = "T";                        03031000
   IF OPCION = "C" THEN                                                           03032000
      GO BACKFORZA710                                                             03033000
   ELSE                                                                           03034000
      IF OPCION = "T" THEN GO FIN                                                 03035000
      ELSE GO PASO710;                                                            03036000
  END;                                                                            03037000
 PASO710:                                                                         03038000
 T710(STATUS = NEVERUSED);                                                        03039000
 JOBATRIBUTOS;                                                                    03040000
 TASKATRIBUTOS;                                                                   03041000
 RUN  #("S151/OBJECT/P710/E" & AUX1/AUX2 ) [T710];                                03042000
 OPTION=(FAULT, ARRAYS, FILES, BDBASE, LIBRARIES, CODE, DSED) ;                   03043000
 VALUE = VALUE710;                                                                03044000
BDNAME = #("S151/LIST/P710/E" & AUX1/AUX2);                                       03045000
FILE TASKFILE (USERBACKUPNAME,TITLE = #("S151/TASKFILE/P710/E"                    03046000
       & AUX1/AUX2/STRING(FECHAY,6)/TIMEDATE(HHMMSS)));                           03047000
ON RESTART, GO BACKFORZA710;                                                      03048000
ON TASKFAULT, GO BACKFORZA710;                                                    03049000
BACKFORZA710:                                                                     03050000
RUN  SYSTEM/FORZA("S151/LIST/P710");                                              03051000
END;                                                                              03052000
                                                                                  03053000

 SUBROUTINE PRODIASNNN(STRING SNNN);                                              03960000
 BEGIN                                                                            03961000
    IF SNNN NEQ "804" AND SNNN NEQ "203" THEN                                     03961010
      BEGIN                                                                       03961020
        VALUE196 := 1;                                                            03961030
        SUB196(SNNN);                                                             03961040
      END;                                                                        03961050
    IF SNNN = "500" THEN                                                          03961100
      BEGIN                                                                       03961200
       SUB172(SNNN);                                                              03961400
       IF NODO =  04 THEN                                                         03961405
       BEGIN                                                                      03961410
        DISPLAY STRING(FECHAY,6);                                                 03961412
        USERP102   := "S151";                                                     03961415
       ARCHIVOP102:= "S151/FILE/E01/INTELLI01/04/" & STRING(FECHAY,6) &           03961420
                     "                                               ";           03961425
        PACKORI102 := "CMEMP            ";                                        03961427
